Plan for "How to build our own": A modern WoW bot typically follows this architecture:

Game State Acquisition:
Internal: Memory reading (fast, risky). Finding offsets (pointers) for Player, Target, NPCs, etc.
External: Screen capture + Image recognition/Perceptual hashing or Lua Addon $\rightarrow$ Pixel Color $\rightarrow$ C# (safer, slower).
Decision Engine:
Finite State Machine (FSM): Simple state transitions (Idle $\rightarrow$ Combat $\rightarrow$ Looting).
Goal-Oriented Action Planning (GOAP): More advanced; defines goals and finds the cheapest sequence of actions to reach them.
Execution/Input:
Virtual Input: SendInput or PostMessage for keys/mouse.
Packet Injection: Sending raw packets to the server (extremely risky, usually detected).
Navigation:
Coordinate-based: Reading X,Y,Z and moving toward a waypoint.
Mesh-based: Using the game's actual navigation mesh (from MPQ files) for A* pathfinding.*

Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (HonorBuddy Style)
MD
Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (HonorBuddy Style)
1. Goal
To build a highly scriptable botting framework where the core engine handles the "how" (movement, combat, memory) and external scripts (profiles) handle the "what" (quests, farming).

2. Technical Stack
Core Engine: C# (.NET 8+).
Scripting Layer: C# Scripting API (Roslyn) or Lua to allow users to write profiles without recompiling the engine.
Memory Access: P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory.
3. Architecture Design
Layer 1: The Core Engine (The Platform)
API Provider: The engine exposes a set of functions to the profiles:
Bot.MoveTo(x, y, z)
Bot.CastSpell("Fireball")
Bot.GetTargetHealth()
Bot.InteractWithObject(guid)
Task Scheduler: A loop that executes the current active profile's logic at a set interval (e.g., every 100ms).
Layer 2: The Behavior System (The Intelligence)
Condition-Action Pairs: Profiles are structured as a list of behaviors:
Behavior 1: If (Health < 30%) 
→
→ Action: Use Health Potion.
Behavior 2: If (Target == null) 
→
→ Action: Find nearest Mob.
Behavior 3: If (InCombat) 
→
→ Action: Execute Class Rotation.
Priority Queue: Behaviors are checked from top to bottom. The first one that matches is executed.
Layer 3: The Profile System (The Content)
Profile Loader: A system to load .cs or .lua files from a /Profiles folder.
Data-Driven Paths: Use JSON or XML files to store lists of waypoints for specific quest lines.
4. Development Roadmap
Phase 1 (API Definition): Define the interface that a "Profile" will use to communicate with the game.
Phase 2 (The Runner): Create the engine loop that can load a script and execute it repeatedly.
Phase 3 (Base Behaviors): Implement a library of standard behaviors (e.g., CombatBehavior, LootBehavior).
Phase 4 (Scripting Integration): Integrate Roslyn or a Lua VM so profiles can be edited in real-time.
Phase 5 (Profile Testing): Create a "Starter Profile" for a level 1 character.
5. Comparison to WRobot Plan
WRobot approach: Integrated, feature-rich, focused on "built-in" tools.
HonorBuddy approach: Framework-first, focused on "extensibility" and user-created scripts.

honorbuddy_research.txt

HonorBuddy - Deep Dive Research

1. OVERVIEW
HonorBuddy (HB) is one of the most historically significant WoW bots. Unlike some bots that are "all-in-one," HonorBuddy functioned more as a framework/engine that users extended through "Profiles" and "Plugins."

2. TECHNICAL ARCHITECTURE
- Framework: C#-based framework.
- Profile-Centric Design: The "soul" of HonorBuddy is the Profile. A profile is a script that tells the bot exactly what to do (e.g., "Go to X, kill Y, talk to Z").
- Behavior System: It uses a "Behavior" system where the bot checks a list of conditions. The first condition that returns "True" is the action the bot takes.
- Modular Plugins: Supports C# plugins that can hook into the bot's core events (e.g., `OnTargetChanged`, `OnCombatStart`).

3. CORE COMPONENTS (Based on GitHub Repository)
- Auth: Handles account authentication and login automation.
- CustomClasses: Class-specific combat logic (e.g., Warlock, Mage) to handle complex rotations.
- Quest Behaviors: Pre-defined logic for interacting with quest NPCs and objects.
- HBRelog: A utility to automatically restart the game and log back in to avoid crashes or disconnects.

4. KEY DIFFERENTIATORS
- Flexibility: Because it is highly scriptable, users could create incredibly complex botting routines that mimicked human players more closely than static bots.
- Community-Driven: The bot's power came from the thousands of community-made profiles available.

5. STATUS (Cataclysm 4.3.4 Version)
- The specific version found on GitHub is tailored for the Cataclysm expansion (4.3.4), commonly used on private servers. It maintains the legacy "Behavior" and "Profile" architecture.

6. SUMMARY
HonorBuddy is less of a "bot" and more of a "botting platform." It provides the low-level tools (memory reading, input, movement) and lets the user (or profile creator) define the high-level intelligence.

Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (WRobot Style)
MD
Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (WRobot Style)
1. Goal
To build a modular automation tool capable of reading game state, making decisions based on a priority system, and simulating user input.

2. Technical Stack
Language: C# (.NET 8+) for the core logic and UI.
Low-Level Interop: C++ DLL for high-speed memory access.
UI: WPF or Blazor (for a modern, reactive dashboard).
Data Format: JSON for profiles and combat rotations.
3. Architecture Design
Layer 1: The "Senses" (Game State Acquisition)
Memory Scanner: A module to read the game's memory.
Key Targets: Local Player (HP, MP, XYZ coords), Current Target (HP, Level, Distance), and the Object Manager (list of all nearby NPCs/Nodes).
Offset Manager: A system to update memory addresses without recompiling the whole bot (via a remote config file).
Lua Integration (Optional/Safer): A companion Lua addon that writes game state to a small area of memory or a log file to reduce direct memory access detection.
Layer 2: The "Brain" (Decision Engine)
Finite State Machine (FSM):
States: IDLE 
→
→ MOVING_TO_TARGET 
→
→ COMBAT 
→
→ LOOTING 
→
→ RECOVERY (Eating/Drinking).
Rotation Engine:
A priority list of spells.
Logic: If (Target.HP < 20% && Spell.Cooldown == 0) 
→
→ Cast "Execute".
Navigation System:
Waypoint-based movement.
Integration with A* Pathfinding using game map data (extracted from MPQ archives).
Layer 3: The "Muscles" (Input Simulation)
Input Driver: Use SendInput or PostMessage to simulate keyboard presses and mouse movements.
Humanizer: Add random delays (e.g., 50ms to 200ms) between actions to avoid "perfect" timing patterns that trigger anti-cheat.
4. Development Roadmap
Phase 1 (Foundations): Create the memory reader and successfully print the player's HP and Coordinates to the console.
Phase 2 (Movement): Implement basic "Move to Coordinate" logic using simulated key presses.
Phase 3 (Combat): Build a simple rotation engine that attacks the nearest target.
Phase 4 (Automation): Implement the FSM to link movement, combat, and looting.
Phase 5 (Interface): Build the GUI to load/save profiles.
5. Risk Mitigation
External Process: Keep the bot as a separate process from the game.
Randomization: Randomize paths, timing, and rotation priorities.
Limited Scope: Start with private servers where anti-cheat is more lenient.

wrobot_research.txt
PLAIN
WRobot - Deep Dive Research

1. OVERVIEW
WRobot is a high-performance automation suite for World of Warcraft, primarily targeting private servers (Vanilla, TBC, MoP, WoD, etc.). It is designed to handle the "grind" of the game by automating repetitive tasks.

2. TECHNICAL ARCHITECTURE
- Language/Framework: Primarily written in .NET (C#), with critical low-level components (like the memory library) rewritten in C++ for performance and reduced latency.
- Data Acquisition: 
    * Memory Reading: Uses Windows APIs (ReadProcessMemory) to read the game's memory addresses. This allows the bot to know exact coordinates, health, target status, and world object locations.
    * Memory Offsets: Relies on "offsets" (specific memory addresses) that change with every game update.
- Navigation:
    * Pathfinding: Utilizes compressed mesh files of the game world to calculate paths.
    * 3D Radar: Renders a live 3D representation of the surrounding area for the user.
- Combat Logic:
    * Fight Classes: A configurable system where users can define rotations (which spell to use under what condition).
    * API: Provides a .NET API for third-party developers to create their own "products" (plugins) for the bot.

3. CORE FEATURES
- Quester: Automates quest acceptance, travel to objectives, and turn-ins.
- Gatherer: Identifies and navigates to mining, herbing, and fishing nodes.
- Grinder: Automates killing mobs in a specific area for XP and loot.
- Battlegrounder: Automates PVP queuing and basic combat in battlegrounds.
- Automaton: A "profile-less" mode where the bot attempts to find its own paths.

4. DETECTION & RISK
- Anti-Cheat (Warden): The bot must constantly fight "Warden," Blizzard's anti-cheat.
- Mitigation: Uses external architectures and "spoofing" to hide its presence from the game process.
- Risk: High. Botting is a violation of ToS and frequently results in permanent account bans.

5. SUMMARY
WRobot is a "Swiss Army Knife" for WoW. Its strength lies in its versatility and the ability for users to extend it via the .NET API and GUI editor.

The user wants me to incorporate "extra information" (which hasn't been explicitly provided in a new message, but they might be referring to the general request to search and find more) and perform a deeper dive.

From the search results, I've found some critical modern trends in WoW botting (2025-2026):

1. The "Pixel Bot" Shift (The Meta for Undetectability):

Many modern bots (like WowClassicGrindBot, Lumen) have completely abandoned memory reading (ReadProcessMemory) and DLL injection because Warden (Blizzard's anti-cheat) has become extremely good at detecting them.
The Strategy:
Senses: Use screen capture (DXGI, Windows Graphics Capture/WGC) to read pixels.
The Trick: Use a Lua addon inside the game that writes game state (coordinates, health, target info) to a specific area of the screen as colored pixels. The external bot then reads these pixels to "know" where it is and what is happening.
Muscles: Use simulated input (PostMessage or hardware-level simulation like Arduino/Raspberry Pi) to send keystrokes.
AI: Some are even using neural networks (like NitroGen) to learn from gameplay videos (RGB $\rightarrow$ Controller Input).
2. Advanced Detection Avoidance:

Hardware Simulation: Using Arduino Leonardo or Raspberry Pi Pico to mimic a real USB keyboard/mouse, bypassing software-level input detection.
External Process: Keeping the bot completely outside the game process.
Kernel Drivers: Writing custom KMDF drivers for input to hide from GetCurrentInputMessageSource (which can tell if an input is simulated).
3. Memory Botting (The High-Risk/High-Reward path):

Still used for "power" bots (like WRobot), but requires:
Pattern Scanning: Using "signatures" (sequences of bytes) to find offsets automatically after game updates, instead of hardcoding them.
Bypassing Warden: Hooking LoadWardenModule or manipulating the scan results to fool the anti-cheat.
4. Technical Stack Trends:

Languages: C# (.NET 10 preview), C++ (for low-level), Python (for AI/OpenCV).
Graphics: DirectX 11 Compute Shaders for fast NPC/color detection on the GPU.
Architecture: Headless servers for pathfinding, Blazor for the UI.

I have conducted a further deep dive into the current state of World of Warcraft automation for 2025-2026. The "meta" for botting has shifted significantly due to the evolution of Blizzard's Warden anti-cheat.

The most critical discovery is the rise of Pixel-Based Automation, which is now the gold standard for avoiding detection.

🆕 New File Created: strategic_comparison.md
This document compares the Memory-Based Path (WRobot/HonorBuddy style) with the Pixel-Based Path (Modern/Undetectable style).

🛠️ Updated Build Plans
I have revised the build plans to include these modern techniques:

For the WRobot-style plan: I added a section on Pattern Scanning, which is how modern memory bots find "offsets" automatically after game updates without needing manual research.
For the HonorBuddy-style plan: I added a "Hybrid" approach, where the framework can support either memory-reading or pixel-reading plugins.
Key Technical Findings from the Deep Dive:
1. The "Pixel Bot" Breakthrough
Modern undetectable bots (like WowClassicGrindBot) no longer touch the game's memory. Instead, they use a two-part system:

The "Eyes" (Lua Addon): A custom in-game Lua addon paints a tiny, nearly invisible area of the screen with specific colors. For example, a specific shade of red might represent the player's health percentage, and a blue pixel might represent the X-coordinate.
The "Brain" (External C# App): The bot uses DXGI or Windows Graphics Capture (WGC) to take high-speed screenshots of those pixels. It decodes the colors back into game data. Because no memory is read and no DLLs are injected, Warden has almost nothing to flag.
2. Hardware-Level Input
To bypass software detection of "simulated" keys (like SendKeys or PostMessage), top-tier developers are now using:

Arduino Leonardo / Raspberry Pi Pico: These devices are recognized by Windows as actual physical USB keyboards. The C# bot sends a command to the Arduino, and the Arduino sends a hardware-level electrical signal to the PC.
3. GPU Acceleration
To detect NPCs and objects without reading memory, modern bots use HLSL Compute Shaders. They process the game screen on the GPU to find specific color patterns (like the gold outline of a lootable object) 100x faster than a CPU could.

4. AI-Driven Automation
There is a emerging trend of using Neural Networks (e.g., the NitroGen project). Instead of writing "If/Then" logic, the bot is trained on hours of human gameplay video to learn how to react to the screen (RGB 
→
→ Input).

💡 Key Insights from the RebornBuddy Forum:
1. The "Platform" vs. "Bot" Distinction
Unlike WRobot, which is a tool you use, HonorBuddy was a platform you built upon. The forum is divided into Plugins and Profiles.

Plugins: Added new capabilities to the engine (e.g., "Adding the ability to track achievements").
Profiles: Used those capabilities to perform specific tasks (e.g., "Farm this specific area for this specific item").
2. The API-First Approach
The existence of a full Doxygen API site shows that HB's core was essentially a massive library of WoW-specific functions. A developer didn't need to know how to read memory; they just called Bot.GetTargetHealth() or Bot.CastSpell("Fireball").

3. The "Death" of Memory Botting
The "DISCONTINUED" status of HB is a direct result of Blizzard's Warden anti-cheat becoming too aggressive against memory-reading and DLL injection. This validates why the Pixel-Based Path (which I researched in the previous step) is the only viable way to build a modern, undetectable bot.

→ Profile" hierarchy.

Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (WRobot Style)
MD
Project Plan: WoW Automation Bot (WRobot Style)
1. Goal
To build a modular automation tool capable of reading game state, making decisions based on a priority system, and simulating user input.

2. Technical Stack
Language: C# (.NET 8+) for the core logic and UI.
Low-Level Interop: C++ DLL for high-speed memory access.
UI: WPF or Blazor (for a modern, reactive dashboard).
Data Format: JSON for profiles and combat rotations.
3. Architecture Design
3. Architecture Design
Layer 1: The "Senses" (Game State Acquisition)
Memory Scanner: A module to read the game's memory.
Target Versions: Special support for 7.3.5 and 8.3.7.
Key Targets: Local Player (HP, MP, XYZ coords, CurrentSpecID, ArtifactXP), Current Target (HP, Level, Distance), and the Object Manager.
Offset Manager: A system to update memory addresses. For 7.3.5, use the identified UnitFields and PlayerFields offsets.
Lua Integration (Optional/Safer): A companion Lua addon that writes game state to a small area of memory or a log file to reduce direct memory access detection.
Layer 2: The "Brain" (Decision Engine)
Finite State Machine (FSM):
States: IDLE 
→
→ MOVING_TO_TARGET 
→
→ COMBAT 
→
→ LOOTING 
→
→ RECOVERY.
Priority Rotation Engine (Modernized):
Instead of a fixed sequence, use a Priority Queue.
Logic: Each spell has a "Requirement" (e.g., TargetHP < 20% or Buff_Proc == True). The bot casts the highest-priority spell whose requirements are met.
Navigation System:
Waypoint-based movement.
Integration with A* Pathfinding using game map data.
Layer 3: The "Muscles" (Input Simulation)
Input Driver: Use SendInput or PostMessage.
Bypass Method: For custom servers, implement RunMacroText simulation for spell casting.
Humanizer: Add random delays (50ms to 200ms) between actions.
4. Development Roadmap
Phase 1 (Foundations): Create the memory reader and successfully print the player's HP and Coordinates to the console.
Phase 2 (Movement): Implement basic "Move to Coordinate" logic using simulated key presses.
Phase 3 (Combat): Build a simple rotation engine that attacks the nearest target.
Phase 4 (Automation): Implement the FSM to link movement, combat, and looting.
Phase 5 (Interface): Build the GUI to load/save profiles.
5. Risk Mitigation
External Process: Keep the bot as a separate process from the game.
Randomization: Randomize paths, timing, and rotation priorities.
Limited Scope: Start with private servers where anti-cheat is more lenient.

modern_versions_research.txt
PLAIN
Modern WoW Versions Research (7.3.5 Legion & 8.3.7 BFA)

1. VERSION TARGETS
- Legion: Version 7.3.5 (Build 26972 / 25946)
- Battle for Azeroth (BFA): Version 8.3.7 (Build 35662)

2. MEMORY ANALYSIS (7.3.5)
Specific offsets have been identified for 7.3.5, allowing for precise state reading. Key memory structures include:
- ObjectFields: GUID, Type, EntryID.
- UnitFields: Health, Power, MaxHealth, MaxPower, Level, AuraState, CombatReach.
- PlayerFields: CurrentSpecID (Crucial for rotations), XP, Coinage, and various combat ratings (Crit, Mastery, Versatility).
- ItemFields: ArtifactXP (Unique to Legion), Durability, StackCount.

3. COMBAT COMPLEXITY (THE "FIGHT CLASS" SYSTEM)
Unlike early WoW versions, Legion and BFA introduced complex combat mechanics:
- Procs: Spells that become available or strengthened based on random events (e.g., "Brain Freeze").
- Artifact Abilities: Unique spells tied to Artifact weapons.
- Resource Management: More complex interactions between primary and secondary resources.

To automate this, a "Priority-Based Rotation Engine" is required:
- Logic: Instead of a sequence (1 $\rightarrow$ 2 $\rightarrow$ 3), the bot must check a list of conditions every tick.
- Example: 
    1. IF (Proc_A == Active) $\rightarrow$ Cast Spell_A
    2. IF (Target_HP < 20%) $\rightarrow$ Cast Execute
    3. IF (Resource > 80%) $\rightarrow$ Cast Dump_Spell
    4. ELSE $\rightarrow$ Cast Filler_Spell

4. CURRENT TOOLING
- WRobot: Explicitly supports both 7.3.5 and 8.3.7. It uses a .NET API and a GUI-based Fight Class editor.
- PQR (Rotation Bot): A legacy but influential tool that focused purely on the rotation aspect using Lua-based conditions.

5. ANTI-CHEAT CONSIDERATIONS (PRIVATE SERVERS)
While Warden is less aggressive on some private servers, many now use "Custom Kernels" or "Modified MPQ files" to block standard Lua functions (like CastSpellByName). 
- Workaround: Use `RunMacroText("/cast Spell Name")` to bypass certain Lua restrictions.

6. SUMMARY
Targeting 7.3.5 and 8.3.7 requires a shift from "Simple Scripting" to "State-Driven Automation." The bot must be able to read a wide array of PlayerFields (Spec, Procs, Resources) and execute a priority-based rotation.

Read: Get the player's Spec, current Buffs (Procs), and Target HP from memory.
Evaluate: Run these values through a priority list (e.g., "Execute" > "Proc Spell" > "Filler").
Act: Send the command via a macro to bypass server-side restrictions.

🧠 PROJECT PSYCHO: Master Automation Blueprint
MD
🧠 PROJECT PSYCHO: Master Automation Blueprint
1. VISION
Project Psycho is a hybrid automation framework for World of Warcraft. It combines the extensibility of a platform (HonorBuddy), the precision of memory-reading (WRobot), and the stealth of pixel-analysis (Modern Bots). It is designed specifically to handle the complexity of versions 7.3.5 (Legion) and 8.3.7 (BFA).

2. HIGH-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE
The system is split into three distinct layers to ensure that the "Brain" never needs to know how the "Senses" or "Muscles" actually work.

Layer A: The Senses (Data Acquisition)
This layer provides the game state to the Brain. It is modular, allowing the user to switch between "Power" and "Stealth" modes.

Mode 1: Power (Memory Reading)
Tech: P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory via a C++ DLL.
Targets: UnitFields, PlayerFields, and ObjectFields (refer to modern_versions_research.txt).
Feature: Pattern Scanning. Instead of hardcoded offsets, the bot scans for opcode patterns to find addresses automatically after game patches.
Mode 2: Stealth (Pixel Sensing)
Tech: DXGI / Windows Graphics Capture (WGC).
Mechanism: A companion Lua addon encodes game state (HP, MP, Coordinates) into a small 10x10 pixel grid of colors on the screen.
Decoding: The C# core reads these pixels and converts colors back into data.
Layer B: The Brain (Decision Engine)
This is where the "Psycho" logic lives. It uses a hierarchical priority system.

The Core API: A library of functions (Bot.Cast(), Bot.MoveTo(), Bot.GetTarget()) that Profiles use to interact with the game.
The Behavior System (HB Style):
Profiles are lists of Behaviors.
Example: IF (Health < 30%) THEN Execute(HealBehavior).
The Priority Rotation Engine (WRobot Style):
Specifically for 7.3.5/8.3.7.
Logic: A priority queue of spells. The bot evaluates requirements (Procs, Cooldowns, Resource levels) and casts the highest-priority valid spell.
The Navigation System:
A* Pathfinding over triangle meshes extracted from MPQ files.
Waypoint-based recording for questing.
Layer C: The Muscles (Input Simulation)
How the bot actually "presses" the keys. This is the primary point of detection.

Software Input: SendInput / PostMessage (Fast, but detectable).
Hardware Input (The Gold Standard): An Arduino Leonardo or Raspberry Pi Pico acting as a USB HID Keyboard. The bot sends a command via Serial/COM port 
→
→ Arduino sends a real electrical keypress.
Bypass Logic: Use RunMacroText("/cast ...") for servers with modified kernels.
3. VERSION-SPECIFIC IMPLEMENTATION (7.3.5 & 8.3.7)
To handle the complexity of Legion and BFA, the bot implements:

Spec-Awareness: The Brain reads the CurrentSpecID to load the correct rotation profile.
Proc Tracking: The Senses track "AuraState" to detect procs, triggering high-priority "Burst" spells in the rotation.
Artifact Integration: Special handling for Artifact-specific abilities and XP tracking.
4. DEVELOPMENT ROADMAP
Phase 1: The Foundation (The "Core")
 Establish the C# project structure.
 Implement the Senses interface (Generic data provider).
 Build the basic Muscles (Software input).
Phase 2: The Senses (Power & Stealth)
 Build the Memory Reader with Pattern Scanning.
 Create the Lua "Pixel-Painter" addon.
 Implement the DXGI screen capture decoder.
Phase 3: The Brain (Logic & Rotations)
 Implement the Finite State Machine (FSM) for general states (IDLE, COMBAT, LOOT).
 Build the Priority Rotation Engine (Requirement 
→
→ Action).
 Create the Profile loader (JSON or C# Scripts).
Phase 4: The Muscles (Hardware & Stealth)
 Develop the Arduino/Pico communication protocol.
 Implement "Humanization" (Randomized delays and curved mouse movement).
 Integrate RunMacroText bypasses.
Phase 5: Integration & Testing
 Test on 7.3.5 and 8.3.7 private servers.
 Stress test the "Behavior" system with a leveling profile.
 Finalize the GUI dashboard for profile management.
5. RISK MITIGATION MATRIX
Risk	Mitigation Strategy
Warden Scan	Use Pixel-Sensing + Hardware Input (Arduino).
Pattern Detection	Randomize timing, paths, and rotation priority.
Game Patch	Use Pattern Scanning instead of static offsets.
Player Report	Implement a "Whisper-Responder" or "Auto-Stop" on interaction.

🧠 PSYCHOBUDDY: Master Automation Blueprint
MD
🧠 PSYCHOBUDDY: Master Automation Blueprint
1. VISION
PsychoBuddy is a hybrid automation framework for World of Warcraft. It combines the extensibility of a platform (HonorBuddy), the precision of memory-reading (WRobot), and the stealth of pixel-analysis (Modern Bots). It is designed specifically to handle the complexity of versions 7.3.5 (Legion) and 8.3.7 (BFA).

2. HIGH-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE
The system is split into three distinct layers to ensure that the "Brain" never needs to know how the "Senses" or "Muscles" actually work.

Layer A: The Senses (Data Acquisition)
This layer provides the game state to the Brain. It is modular, allowing the user to switch between "Power" and "Stealth" modes.

Mode 1: Power (Memory Reading)
Tech: P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory via a C++ DLL.
Targets: UnitFields, PlayerFields, and ObjectFields (refer to modern_versions_research.txt).
Feature: Pattern Scanning. Instead of hardcoded offsets, the bot scans for opcode patterns to find addresses automatically after game patches.
Mode 2: Stealth (Pixel Sensing)
Tech: DXGI / Windows Graphics Capture (WGC).
Mechanism: A companion Lua addon encodes game state (HP, MP, Coordinates) into a small 10x10 pixel grid of colors on the screen.
Decoding: The C# core reads these pixels and converts colors back into data.
Layer B: The Brain (Decision Engine)
This is where the "Psycho" logic lives. It uses a hierarchical priority system.

The Core API: A library of functions (Bot.Cast(), Bot.MoveTo(), Bot.GetTarget()) that Profiles use to interact with the game.
The Behavior System (HB Style):
Profiles are lists of Behaviors.
Example: IF (Health < 30%) THEN Execute(HealBehavior).
The Priority Rotation Engine (WRobot Style):
Specifically for 7.3.5/8.3.7.
Logic: A priority queue of spells. The bot evaluates requirements (Procs, Cooldowns, Resource levels) and casts the highest-priority valid spell.
The Navigation System:
A* Pathfinding over triangle meshes extracted from MPQ files.
Waypoint-based recording for questing.
Layer C: The Muscles (Input Simulation)
How the bot actually "presses" the keys. This is the primary point of detection.

Software Input: SendInput / PostMessage (Fast, but detectable).
Hardware Input (The Gold Standard): An Arduino Leonardo or Raspberry Pi Pico acting as a USB HID Keyboard. The bot sends a command via Serial/COM port 
→
→ Arduino sends a real electrical keypress.
Bypass Logic: Use RunMacroText("/cast ...") for servers with modified kernels.
3. VERSION-SPECIFIC IMPLEMENTATION (7.3.5 & 8.3.7)
To handle the complexity of Legion and BFA, the bot implements:

Spec-Awareness: The Brain reads the CurrentSpecID to load the correct rotation profile.
Proc Tracking: The Senses track "AuraState" to detect procs, triggering high-priority "Burst" spells in the rotation.
Artifact Integration: Special handling for Artifact-specific abilities and XP tracking.
4. DEVELOPMENT ROADMAP
Phase 1: The Foundation (The "Core")
 Establish the C# project structure.
 Implement the Senses interface (Generic data provider).
 Build the basic Muscles (Software input).
Phase 2: The Senses (Power & Stealth)
 Build the Memory Reader with Pattern Scanning.
 Create the Lua "Pixel-Painter" addon.
 Implement the DXGI screen capture decoder.
Phase 3: The Brain (Logic & Rotations)
 Implement the Finite State Machine (FSM) for general states (IDLE, COMBAT, LOOT).
 Build the Priority Rotation Engine (Requirement 
→
→ Action).
 Create the Profile loader (JSON or C# Scripts).
Phase 4: The Muscles (Hardware & Stealth)
 Develop the Arduino/Pico communication protocol.
 Implement "Humanization" (Randomized delays and curved mouse movement).
 Integrate RunMacroText bypasses.
Phase 5: Integration & Testing
 Test on 7.3.5 and 8.3.7 private servers.
 Stress test the "Behavior" system with a leveling profile.
 Finalize the GUI dashboard for profile management.
5. RISK MITIGATION MATRIX
Risk	Mitigation Strategy
Warden Scan	Use Pixel-Sensing + Hardware Input (Arduino).
Pattern Detection	Randomize timing, paths, and rotation priority.
Game Patch	Use Pattern Scanning instead of static offsets.
Player Report	Implement a "Whisper-Responder" or "Auto-Stop" on interaction.

🧠 PsychoBuddy
PsychoBuddy is a professional-grade, hybrid automation platform for World of Warcraft. It is designed to provide a highly flexible, scriptable, and powerful experience for users who want to automate their gameplay on private and personal servers.

Combining the best of historical botting frameworks with 2026's most advanced stealth techniques, PsychoBuddy offers a "Power" mode for precision and a "Stealth" mode for maximum security.

⚠️ CRITICAL WARNING & DISCLAIMER
READ THIS BEFORE USE:

OFFICIAL SERVERS PROHIBITED: The use of PsychoBuddy on official Blizzard Entertainment servers is STRICTLY PROHIBITED. This software is intended exclusively for use on approved private servers and personal/local servers.
RISK OF BANS: Botting is a violation of the Terms of Service of almost every game server. While PsychoBuddy implements advanced anti-detection measures, no bot is 100% undetectable.
NO LIABILITY: The author of PsychoBuddy is NOT liable for any account bans, suspensions, data loss, or hardware issues resulting from the use of this software. Use this tool at your own risk.
EDUCATIONAL USE: This project is shared for development, evaluation, and educational purposes regarding game automation and memory architecture.
🛠️ Key Features
👁️ The Senses (Hybrid Data Acquisition)
PsychoBuddy doesn't rely on just one method to see the game. You can choose your level of risk:

Power Mode (Memory): Direct memory reading for frame-perfect precision. Includes advanced Pattern Scanning to automatically find offsets after game updates.
Stealth Mode (Pixels): A "Zero-Touch" approach using a companion Lua addon and high-speed screen capture (DXGI/WGC). No memory tampering, no DLL injection.
🧠 The Brain (Adaptive Logic)
Priority Rotation Engine: specifically optimized for Legion (7.3.5) and BFA (8.3.7). It handles complex procs, resource management, and artifact abilities using a requirement-based priority queue.
Behavioral Framework: A modular system allowing for complex "If-Then" logic (e.g., "If health < 30% and in combat, cast Heal").
A Navigation:* High-precision movement using triangle meshes for seamless world navigation.
💪 The Muscles (Input Simulation)
Software Input: Standard simulated keypresses for basic tasks.
Hardware Integration: Full support for Arduino Leonardo and Raspberry Pi Pico for hardware-level USB HID input simulation, bypassing most software-based anti-cheat detectors.
Humanization: Randomized timing and movement curves to mimic real human input patterns.
🎮 Supported Versions
PsychoBuddy is currently optimized for:

Legion (7.3.5)
Battle for Azeroth (8.3.7)
(Support for other versions may be available via community profiles)

🧠 PsychoBuddy
PsychoBuddy is a professional-grade, hybrid automation platform for World of Warcraft. It provides a highly flexible and powerful environment for automating gameplay, specifically designed for high-complexity expansions.

Unlike basic scripts, PsychoBuddy is a full-featured platform that combines the precision of memory-reading with the invisibility of pixel-analysis, allowing users to choose the perfect balance between power and security.

🛠️ What PsychoBuddy Does
👁️ Adaptive Senses (Data Acquisition)
PsychoBuddy allows you to choose how the bot "sees" the game world:

Power Mode (Memory): Uses high-speed memory reading and Pattern Scanning to track player and NPC states with frame-perfect accuracy.
Stealth Mode (Pixels): A "Zero-Touch" system that uses a companion Lua addon to encode game data into pixels, which are then read by the bot via DXGI/WGC. This eliminates memory tampering and DLL injection.
🧠 The Decision Brain (Adaptive Logic)
Priority Rotation Engine: Engineered for Legion (7.3.5) and BFA (8.3.7). It uses a requirement-based priority queue to handle complex procs, resource management, and artifact abilities.
Behavioral Framework: A modular "If-Then" system that allows for complex logic (e.g., "If health < 30% 
→
→ Use Healthstone 
→
→ Cast Shield").

The laest research (WowClassicGrindBot and others) mentioned "Multi-instance support."

WowClassicGrindBot used Windows Graphics Capture (WGC) to capture a specific window rather than the entire desktop.

Memory reading (ReadProcessMemory) requires a process handle. If multiple clients are running, each has a different Process ID (PID).

Input simulation (SendInput vs PostMessage). SendInput is global (affects the foreground window). PostMessage can target a specific window handle (HWND) in the background.

Confirm: Yes, the design is intended to control the user's game client externally (not a server-side bot).

Explain Multi-Client Support:

PID Mapping: The bot will need to map a "Profile" to a specific Process ID (PID). Instead of just looking for "Wow.exe", it will allow the user to select which instance they are controlling.
Window Targeting: For "Stealth Mode" (Pixel reading), it will use WGC (Windows Graphics Capture) to target a specific window handle, allowing it to "see" Client A and Client B independently.
Background Input: To support two clients, it cannot rely solely on SendInput (which requires the window to be in focus). It must use PostMessage or a similar method to send keys to the background window, or a sequential "Switch $\rightarrow$ Action $\rightarrow$ Switch" logic.
Reassure the user.

Confirm technical feasibility.

State exactly how it will be implemented.

Yes, that is correct.

PsychoBuddy is designed as an external controller. It does not live inside the game server; it lives on the user's computer and "drives" the game client by reading its state and simulating a human player's inputs.

How we will handle two clients on one computer:
To support multiple instances (Multi-boxing), I will implement the following technical architecture:

1. Process ID (PID) Mapping
Instead of just searching for "Wow.exe," PsychoBuddy will identify every running instance of the game. The user will be able to assign a specific Profile to a specific Process ID.

Example: Profile A 
→
→ PID 1234 (Window 1), Profile B 
→
→ PID 5678 (Window 2).
2. Targeted Senses (Window-Specific Capture)

For Power Mode: The bot will use the specific PID to read memory only from that instance.
For Stealth Mode: I will use Windows Graphics Capture (WGC). Unlike old screen-scrapers that just look at the "top" window, WGC can capture a specific window handle (HWND) even if it is partially covered or in the background. This allows the bot to "see" Client A and Client B separately and simultaneously.
3. Targeted Muscles (Background Input)
To control two clients without constantly switching windows (which is slow and looks suspicious), we will use PostMessage or SendMessage APIs. These allow the bot to send keystrokes and mouse clicks directly to a specific window's message queue, meaning the bot can control a client even if it is not the foreground window.

Summary:
The system will be fully "Instance-Aware." It will treat each game client as a separate entity with its own memory space, its own screen capture stream, and its own input queue.

I have noted this requirement in the architecture. I am now standing by for your next step.


Rather then the user having to find the pid, can we make it so you just drag the already open wow client into psychobuddy or, you direct psychobuddy to you're wow.exe and open them inside psychobuddy? What option is best? Otherwise how do other services do this?



Thought for 5 seconds

The user wants to simplify how the bot attaches to the game client. They don't want the user to manually find a PID (Process ID). * Option A: Drag-and-drop the open game client window into the bot. * Option B: Direct the bot to the wow.exe and have the bot launch the game. * User Question: Which is best? How do other services do it?

WRobot/HonorBuddy/Modern Bots: They typically use a "Dropdown List." The bot scans all running processes for "Wow.exe" and lists them (e.g., "WoW Client - [CharacterName]").
Launchers: Some bots act as the launcher, starting the game process and automatically attaching to it upon launch.

Handle-based attachment: Many use FindWindow or EnumWindows to get a list of windows with "World of Warcraft" in the title.

Drag and Drop: Very intuitive, but technically tricky with admin rights. If the game is running as Admin (common for bots) and the bot is not, or vice versa, drag-and-drop might fail or requires specific shell hooks.

Direct Launch (The "Launcher" method): Excellent for control. The bot knows exactly when the game starts, can inject a "Launcher" if needed, and has the PID immediately. However, it might conflict with official launchers (Battle.net).

The "Process List" (The Industry Standard): The bot scans for all Wow.exe processes and presents them in a dropdown. The user just clicks the one they want.

Recommendation: A combination of Automatic Detection (Dropdown) and Direct Launch (Optional).

Why: It's the most robust. If the user already has the game open, they just pick it from the list. If they want the bot to handle it, they click "Launch."

Technical Detail for Multi-Client:* Since the user wants two clients, the bot needs to distinguish between them. A simple list of "Wow.exe" isn't enough. The bot should read the Window Title (which usually contains the character name or realm) to help the user identify which client is which.
Confirm that manual PID entry is old-school and not needed.

Explain the "Industry Standard" (Process Scanning/Dropdown).

Evaluate the user's suggestions (Drag & Drop vs. Launcher).

Propose the "Smart Attachment" system.

Manual PID entry is definitely "old school" and a poor user experience. Modern professional software almost never asks the user for a PID.

To answer your question: The "Industry Standard" is a combination of Process Scanning and Window Title detection.

Here is how the top services (like WRobot and modern pixel-bots) handle this, and my recommendation for PsychoBuddy.

1. How other services do it:
Most bots use a "Client Selector" dropdown menu. When the program starts, it does the following in the background:

Scan: It looks for all running processes named Wow.exe.
Identify: For every Wow.exe it finds, it looks at the Window Title.
Example: Instead of "Wow.exe (PID 1234)", the user sees "World of Warcraft - [Character: Thrall]".
Select: The user simply clicks the character/window they want to attach to from the dropdown.
2. Evaluating your ideas:
Option A: Drag-and-Drop

The Vibe: Very modern and intuitive.
The Problem: It is technically difficult to implement reliably. Because WoW often runs with Administrator privileges, Windows prevents "Drag-and-Drop" from a high-privilege window (the game) to a lower-privilege window (the bot) for security reasons. It often results in the "forbidden" circle icon.
Option B: Direct Launch (wow.exe)

The Vibe: Very convenient. The bot acts as the launcher.
The Problem: It can conflict with the official Battle.net launcher. If the user needs the Battle.net launcher to authenticate their account, a direct launch of wow.exe might trigger a "Please launch via Battle.net" error.
3. My Recommendation: The "Smart Attachment" System
For PsychoBuddy, I recommend a three-tier system that covers every possible user preference:

Tier 1: The Automatic Selector (Primary Method)
The main screen has a dropdown menu. The bot scans for all Wow.exe instances and lists them by Window Title (Character Name).

User Experience: Open game 
→
→ Open PsychoBuddy 
→
→ Select "Character A" from the list 
→
→ Click "Attach."
Tier 2: The "Launch & Attach" (Convenience Method)
A button that says "Launch Game." The bot starts the wow.exe process, waits for it to initialize, and automatically attaches to it the moment the window appears.

User Experience: Open PsychoBuddy 
→
→ Click "Launch" 
→
→ Bot handles the rest.
Tier 3: The "Manual Path" (Fallback Method)
A settings option where the user can browse to their wow.exe location once, so the bot knows exactly where the executable is for the "Launch" feature.

Why this is best for your "Two Clients" requirement:
Since you want to support two clients, the Window Title method is the only way to make it easy. If the user has two clients open, they will see:

[Window] World of Warcraft - Character A
[Window] World of Warcraft - Character B
They can then simply assign a different profile to each window in the PsychoBuddy dashboard.

Does this "Smart Attachment" approach sound right to you, or would you still prefer to try and implement the Drag-and-Drop feel?

🖇️ PsychoBuddy: Attachment Module Specification
MD
🖇️ PsychoBuddy: Attachment Module Specification
1. Objective
To provide a seamless, user-friendly way to connect PsychoBuddy to one or more World of Warcraft game clients, regardless of whether the executable is named standardly or has been modded/renamed.

2. The Attachment Workflow
Step 1: Process Discovery (The Auto-Scan)
The bot will periodically scan for running processes.

Primary Target: Wow.exe
Logic:
Use System.Diagnostics.Process.GetProcesses() to retrieve all running tasks.
Filter for processes matching the target name.
For each match, use User32.dll's GetWindowText to retrieve the window title.
UI Presentation: Display a list to the user: "World of Warcraft - [Character Name]".
Step 2: Fallback Identification (The Custom Path)
If the user is using a modded client (e.g., wow8.3.7.exe), the auto-scan may fail.

User Configuration: A "Browse for Executable" button in the settings.
Storage: The path is saved to settings.json.
Logic:
The bot checks the saved custom path.
If a process is running from that specific path, it is added to the "Available Clients" list regardless of the .exe name.
Step 3: Binding & Multi-boxing
Once a client is selected from the list, PsychoBuddy creates a Binding.

Binding Data:
ProcessID (PID): For memory reading and process management.
WindowHandle (HWND): For sending targeted inputs (PostMessage) and capturing specific window pixels (WGC).
ProfileID: Which .json profile is controlling this specific window.
3. Technical Implementation Details
API Requirements (Windows User32.dll)
To implement this, the following Win32 API calls are required:

EnumWindows: To iterate through all open windows.
GetWindowThreadProcessId: To link a window handle to a PID.
GetWindowText: To read the "World of Warcraft - [Character]" string.
IsWindowVisible: To ensure we don't try to attach to hidden background processes.
Multi-Instance Handling
The system will support a List<ClientBinding> structure:

csharp

public class ClientBinding {
    public int Pid { get; set; }
    public IntPtr WindowHandle { get; set; }
    public string CharacterName { get; set; }
    public string AssignedProfile { get; set; }
    public BotStatus Status { get; set; } // Attached, Running, Stopped
}
4. User Interface (UI) Design
The Selector: A dropdown menu showing "Available Clients."
The Manager: A grid showing all "Attached Clients," their current profile, and a Start/Stop toggle for each.
The Settings: A "Custom Executable Path" field with a file browser.

🧠 PSYCHOBUDDY: Master Automation Blueprint
MD
🧠 PSYCHOBUDDY: Master Automation Blueprint
1. VISION
PsychoBuddy is a hybrid automation framework for World of Warcraft. It combines the extensibility of a platform (HonorBuddy), the precision of memory-reading (WRobot), and the stealth of pixel-analysis (Modern Bots). It is designed specifically to handle the complexity of versions 7.3.5 (Legion) and 8.3.7 (BFA).

2. HIGH-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE
The system is split into three distinct layers to ensure that the "Brain" never needs to know how the "Senses" or "Muscles" actually work.

Layer A: The Senses (Data Acquisition)
This layer provides the game state to the Brain. It is modular, allowing the user to switch between "Power" and "Stealth" modes.

Smart Attachment System:
Auto-Scan: Scans for Wow.exe and identifies instances via Window Titles (Character Names).
Fallback Pathing: Allows users to manually specify a custom executable path (e.g., wow8.3.7.exe) if the client is renamed or modded.
Instance Mapping: Maps specific Window Handles (HWND) and PIDs to independent Bot Profiles, enabling multi-boxing.
Mode 1: Power (Memory Reading)
Tech: P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory via a C++ DLL.
Targets: UnitFields, PlayerFields, and ObjectFields (refer to modern_versions_research.txt).
Feature: Pattern Scanning. Instead of hardcoded offsets, the bot scans for opcode patterns to find addresses automatically after game patches.
Mode 2: Stealth (Pixel Sensing)
Tech: DXGI / Windows Graphics Capture (WGC).
Mechanism: A companion Lua addon encodes game state (HP, MP, Coordinates) into a small 10x10 pixel grid of colors on the screen.
Decoding: The C# core reads these pixels and converts colors back into data.
Layer B: The Brain (Decision Engine)
This is where the "Psycho" logic lives. It uses a hierarchical priority system.

The Core API: A library of functions (Bot.Cast(), Bot.MoveTo(), Bot.GetTarget()) that Profiles use to interact with the game.
The Behavior System (HB Style):
Profiles are lists of Behaviors.
Example: IF (Health < 30%) THEN Execute(HealBehavior).
The Priority Rotation Engine (WRobot Style):
Specifically for 7.3.5/8.3.7.
Logic: A priority queue of spells. The bot evaluates requirements (Procs, Cooldowns, Resource levels) and casts the highest-priority valid spell.
The Navigation System:
A* Pathfinding over triangle meshes extracted from MPQ files.
Waypoint-based recording for questing.
Layer C: The Muscles (Input Simulation)
How the bot actually "presses" the keys. This is the primary point of detection.

Software Input: SendInput / PostMessage (Fast, but detectable).
Hardware Input (The Gold Standard): An Arduino Leonardo or Raspberry Pi Pico acting as a USB HID Keyboard. The bot sends a command via Serial/COM port 
→
→ Arduino sends a real electrical keypress.
Bypass Logic: Use RunMacroText("/cast ...") for servers with modified kernels.
3. VERSION-SPECIFIC IMPLEMENTATION (7.3.5 & 8.3.7)
To handle the complexity of Legion and BFA, the bot implements:

Spec-Awareness: The Brain reads the CurrentSpecID to load the correct rotation profile.
Proc Tracking: The Senses track "AuraState" to detect procs, triggering high-priority "Burst" spells in the rotation.
Artifact Integration: Special handling for Artifact-specific abilities and XP tracking.
4. DEVELOPMENT ROADMAP
Phase 1: The Foundation (The "Core")
 Establish the C# project structure.
 Implement the Senses interface (Generic data provider).
 Build the basic Muscles (Software input).
Phase 2: The Senses (Power & Stealth)
 Build the Memory Reader with Pattern Scanning.
 Create the Lua "Pixel-Painter" addon.
 Implement the DXGI screen capture decoder.
Phase 3: The Brain (Logic & Rotations)
 Implement the Finite State Machine (FSM) for general states (IDLE, COMBAT, LOOT).
 Build the Priority Rotation Engine (Requirement 
→
→ Action).
 Create the Profile loader (JSON or C# Scripts).
Phase 4: The Muscles (Hardware & Stealth)
 Develop the Arduino/Pico communication protocol.
 Implement "Humanization" (Randomized delays and curved mouse movement).
 Integrate RunMacroText bypasses.
Phase 5: Integration & Testing
 Test on 7.3.5 and 8.3.7 private servers.
 Stress test the "Behavior" system with a leveling profile.
 Finalize the GUI dashboard for profile management.
5. RISK MITIGATION MATRIX
Risk	Mitigation Strategy
Warden Scan	Use Pixel-Sensing + Hardware Input (Arduino).
Pattern Detection	Randomize timing, paths, and rotation priority.
Game Patch	Use Pattern Scanning instead of static offsets.
Player Report	Implement a "Whisper-Responder" or "Auto-Stop" on interaction.

🧠 PSYCHOBUDDY: Master Automation Blueprint
1. VISION
PsychoBuddy is a professional-grade multi-boxing and automation platform for World of Warcraft. Its primary purpose is to allow a user to manage a "fleet" of characters—typically allowing the user to play one main account manually while PsychoBuddy autonomously controls and synchronizes multiple other clients (e.g., 4+ bot accounts) in the background.

It combines the extensibility of a platform (HonorBuddy), the precision of memory-reading (WRobot), and the stealth of pixel-analysis (Modern Bots), designed specifically for the complexity of versions 7.3.5 (Legion) and 8.3.7 (BFA).

2. HIGH-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE
The system is split into three distinct layers to ensure that the "Brain" never needs to know how the "Senses" or "Muscles" actually work.

Layer A: The Senses (Data Acquisition)
This layer provides the game state to the Brain. It is modular, allowing the user to switch between "Power" and "Stealth" modes.

Smart Attachment System:
Auto-Scan: Scans for Wow.exe and identifies instances via Window Titles (Character Names).
Fallback Pathing: Allows users to manually specify a custom executable path (e.g., wow8.3.7.exe) if the client is renamed or modded.
Instance Mapping: Maps specific Window Handles (HWND) and PIDs to independent Bot Profiles, enabling multi-boxing.
Mode 1: Power (Memory Reading)
Tech: P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory via a C++ DLL.
Targets: UnitFields, PlayerFields, and ObjectFields (refer to modern_versions_research.txt).
Feature: Pattern Scanning. Instead of hardcoded offsets, the bot scans for opcode patterns to find addresses automatically after game patches.
Mode 2: Stealth (Pixel Sensing)
Tech: DXGI / Windows Graphics Capture (WGC).
Mechanism: A companion Lua addon encodes game state (HP, MP, Coordinates) into a small 10x10 pixel grid of colors on the screen.
Decoding: The C# core reads these pixels and converts colors back into data.
Layer B: The Brain (Decision Engine)
This is where the "Psycho" logic lives. It uses a hierarchical priority system.

The Core API: A library of functions (Bot.Cast(), Bot.MoveTo(), Bot.GetTarget()) that Profiles use to interact with the game.
The Behavior System (HB Style):
Profiles are lists of Behaviors.
Example: IF (Health < 30%) THEN Execute(HealBehavior).
The Priority Rotation Engine (WRobot Style):
Specifically for 7.3.5/8.3.7.
Logic: A priority queue of spells. The bot evaluates requirements (Procs, Cooldowns, Resource levels) and casts the highest-priority valid spell.
The Navigation System:
A* Pathfinding over triangle meshes extracted from MPQ files.
Waypoint-based recording for questing.
Layer C: The Muscles (Input Simulation)
How the bot actually "presses" the keys. Since PsychoBuddy is designed for multi-boxing, the priority is Background Control.

Background Input (The Multi-box Engine):
Use PostMessage and SendMessage (Win32 API) to send keystrokes and mouse clicks directly to the target window's message queue.
This allows the bot to control 4+ clients in the background while the user interacts with their main client in the foreground.
Hardware Integration (Anti-Cheat Bypass):
Native support for Arduino Leonardo and Raspberry Pi Pico for true USB HID hardware-level input simulation.
Note: Hardware input typically targets the foreground window; for multi-boxing, this is used for the "Main" account or for specific "Active" botting sessions.
Software Simulation: High-performance simulated keypresses for basic tasks.
Humanization: Randomized timing and curved movement to mimic real human behavior.
3. VERSION-SPECIFIC IMPLEMENTATION (7.3.5 & 8.3.7)
To handle the complexity of Legion and BFA, the bot implements:

Spec-Awareness: The Brain reads the CurrentSpecID to load the correct rotation profile.
Proc Tracking: The Senses track "AuraState" to detect procs, triggering high-priority "Burst" spells in the rotation.
Artifact Integration: Special handling for Artifact-specific abilities and XP tracking.
4. DEVELOPMENT ROADMAP
Phase 1: The Foundation (The "Core")
 Establish the C# project structure.
 Implement the Senses interface (Generic data provider).
 Build the basic Muscles (Software input).
Phase 2: The Senses (Power & Stealth)
 Build the Memory Reader with Pattern Scanning.
 Create the Lua "Pixel-Painter" addon.
 Implement the DXGI screen capture decoder.
Phase 3: The Brain (Logic & Rotations)
 Implement the Finite State Machine (FSM) for general states (IDLE, COMBAT, LOOT).
 Build the Priority Rotation Engine (Requirement 
→
→ Action).
 Create the Profile loader (JSON or C# Scripts).
Phase 4: The Muscles (Hardware & Stealth)
 Develop the Arduino/Pico communication protocol.
 Implement "Humanization" (Randomized delays and curved mouse movement).
 Integrate RunMacroText bypasses.
Phase 5: Integration & Testing
 Test on 7.3.5 and 8.3.7 private servers.
 Stress test the "Behavior" system with a leveling profile.
 Finalize the GUI dashboard for profile management.
5. RISK MITIGATION MATRIX
Risk	Mitigation Strategy
Warden Scan	Use Pixel-Sensing + Hardware Input (Arduino).
Pattern Detection	Randomize timing, paths, and rotation priority.
Game Patch	Use Pattern Scanning instead of static offsets.
Player Report	Implement a "Whisper-Responder" or "Auto-Stop" on interaction.

warrior_bfa.txt
PLAIN
BFA WARRIOR (8.3.7) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
SPEC: ARMS (PVE)
========================================================================
PRIORITY LIST (Single Target):
1. Colossus Smash: Maintain uptime.
2. Rend: Maintain bleed.
3. Mortal Strike: Primary damage.
4. Overpower: Use on proc.
5. Execute: Use when target < 35% (depending on talents).
6. Slam: Rage dump / filler.

PRIORITY LIST (AoE):
1. Sweeping Strikes: Essential for multi-target.
2. Ravager: Primary AoE burst.
3. Cleave: High priority in multi-target.
4. Whirlwind: Use on cooldown.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Shattered Defenses: Triggered by Colossus Smash.
- Tactician: Proc that reduces cost of next ability.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avatar: Burst window.
- Bladestorm: High AoE damage.

========================================================================
SPEC: FURY (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Rage $\rightarrow$ Rampage $\rightarrow$ Enrage.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Rampage: Maintains Enrage.
2. Marked for Execution: Execute when stacks are full.
3. Stormblast: Use if talented and procs.
4. Raging Blow: Primary generator.
5. Bloodthirst: High priority filler.
6. Whirlwind/Slam: Fillers.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Enrage: Triggered by Rampage.
- Sudden Death: Proc for instant Execute.

COOLDOWNS:
- Recklessness: Huge burst.
- Avatar: Strength increase.

========================================================================
SPEC: PROTECTION (PVE)
========================================================================
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Shield Slam: Top priority.
2. Revenge: Use on proc.
3. Thunder Clap: AoE upkeep.
4. Devastate: Filler / Sunder application.
5. Shield Block: Active mitigation.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Sword and Board: Resets Shield Slam.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL WARRIOR)
========================================================================
- Opener: Charge -> Stun (Storm Bolt/Shockwave) -> Burst (Avatar/Recklessness).
- Priority: Interrupt casts with Pummel immediately.
- Arms PvP: Focus on "Mortal Wounds" uptime to reduce enemy healing.
- Fury PvP: High pressure, constant attacks to prevent enemy recovery.
- Prot PvP: Focus on disrupting the enemy's target and using high CC.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-120)
========================================================================
- Level 1-10: Charge, Rend, Basic Attacks.
- Level 10-120:
    - Arms/Fury: Fast questing, high AoE.
    - Protection: Tanking for fast dungeon queues.
- General Loop: Charge -> Rend -> Priority Attack -> Execute.
- Use "Ignore Pain" or "Die by the Sword" to chain pulls without stopping to heal.

legion_warrior.txt
PLAIN
LEGION WARRIOR (7.3.5) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
SPEC: ARMS (PVE)
========================================================================
PRIORITY LIST (Single Target):
1. Colossus Smash: Maintain 100% uptime.
2. Rend: Maintain debuff (refresh < 5s remaining).
3. Mortal Strike: Use on cooldown.
4. Overpower: Use on "Taste for Blood" procs.
5. Execute: Use when target < 20% HP.
6. Slam: Rage dump when above 60 Rage and others are on CD.

PRIORITY LIST (AoE/Multi-Target):
1. Talent: Sweeping Strikes (if targets > 2).
2. Warbreaker: High priority AoE burst.
3. Bladestorm: Use when targets are stacked.
4. Cleave: Use instead of Heroic Strike.
5. Whirlwind: Use on cooldown for AoE.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Shattered Defenses: Applied by Colossus Smash/Warbreaker. Increases crit chance of Mortal Strike.
- Taste for Blood: Procs Overpower.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avatar: Massive strength increase, use with Colossus Smash.
- Battle Cry: Use on cooldown to increase damage.

========================================================================
SPEC: FURY (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Enrage (Increases damage and reduces GCD).
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Enraged Raging Blow: Top priority.
2. Rampage: Used to trigger/maintain Enrage.
3. Bloodthirst: Primary generator/filler.
4. Non-Enraged Raging Blow: Use if Enrage is not active.
5. Furious Slash: Filler.
6. Execute: Use when target < 20% HP.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Enrage: Triggered by Rampage.
- Sudden Death: Proc that allows an instant Execute.

COOLDOWNS:
- Recklessness: Guaranteed crits. Use with Avatar.
- Odyn's Fury: High damage burst.

========================================================================
SPEC: PROTECTION (PVE)
========================================================================
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Shield Slam: Top priority (Generates high threat).
2. Revenge: Use on every proc (Sword and Board).
3. Thunder Clap: Maintain AoE threat/slow.
4. Devastate: Filler and Sunder Armor application.
5. Shield Block: Maintain uptime for mitigation.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Sword and Board: Proc that resets Shield Slam cooldown.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL WARRIOR)
========================================================================
- Opener: Charge/Intercept -> Stun (Shockwave/Storm Bolt) -> Burst.
- Interrupts: Pummel is top priority against casters.
- Utility: Disarm (especially against melee/rogues), Intimidating Shout (last resort CC).
- Arms PvP: Maintain Rend -> Mortal Strike -> Overpower. Use "Shattered Defenses" for burst.
- Fury PvP: High burst with Avatar/Recklessness. Focus on target priority and keeping target slow (Hamstring).
- Prot PvP: High survival, use Shockwave for group CC.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-110)
========================================================================
- Level 1-10: Basic attacks, Charge (Lvl 1), Rend.
- Level 10+: Switch to Protection for "Easy Mode" (higher survivability).
- General Loop: Charge -> Rend -> Mortal Strike (Arms) / Raging Blow (Fury) -> Execute.
- Use "Die by the Sword" or "Ignore Pain" when health < 40% to avoid downtime.

BFA MAGE (8.3.7) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
TALENT-DEPENDENT ABILITIES (CHECK BEFORE CASTING)
========================================================================
- Sunfury Abilities: Check if Sunfury hero spec is active (Arcane Phoenix).
- Frostfire Abilities: Check if Frostfire hero spec is active (Frostfire Bolt).
- Shimmer / Alter Time: Check active talent tree.
- Comet Storm / Glacial Spike: Check Frost talent tree.

========================================================================
SPEC: ARCANE (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Mana management and Arcane Charge stacks.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Arcane Power / Rune of Power: Use for burst windows.
2. Arcane Blast: Build to 4 charges.
3. Arcane Missiles: Use on procs (Clearcasting).
4. Arcane Barrage: Dump charges and deal AoE.
5. Arcane Explosion: Use for multi-target charge building.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Clearcasting: Procs Arcane Missiles.

COOLDOWNS:
- Arcane Power: Major burst.
- Presence of Mind: Instant cast.

========================================================================
SPEC: FIRE (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Hot Streak and Combustion.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Combustion: Primary burst.
2. Pyroblast: Use during Hot Streak.
3. Fire Blast: Generate Hot Streak.
4. Frostfire Bolt: Primary filler.
5. Scorch: Use when moving.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Hot Streak: Triggered by crits.

COOLDOWNS:
- Combustion: Guaranteed crits.

========================================================================
SPEC: FROST (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Splinter generation and Glacial Spike shatters.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Frozen Orb: Use on cooldown.
2. Ray of Frost / Comet Storm: High priority burst.
3. Flurry: Use with Brain Freeze proc.
4. Ice Lance: Use with Fingers of Frost.
5. Glacial Spike: Use when charges are full and target is chilled.
6. Frostbolt: Filler.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Fingers of Frost: Procs Ice Lance.
- Brain Freeze: Procs Flurry.

COOLDOWNS:
- Icy Veins: Primary burst.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL MAGE)
========================================================================
- Opener: Root/Slow $\rightarrow$ Burst.
- Utility: Counterspell, Polymorph, Blink/Shimmer.
- Arcane PvP: Focus on burst and distance.
- Fire PvP: High burst and pressure.
- Frost PvP: Maximum control and kiting.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-120)
========================================================================
- Level 1-10: Basic spells.
- Level 10-120:
    - Frost: Best for solo questing (AoE and survivability).
    - Fire/Arcane: Higher single-target burst but more fragile.
- General Loop: Frostbolt $\rightarrow$ Ice Lance $\rightarrow$ Flurry.
- Use Mirror Image and Ice Block to survive high-pulls.

LEGION MAGE (7.3.5) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
TALENT-DEPENDENT ABILITIES (CHECK BEFORE CASTING)
========================================================================
- Rune of Power: Requires talent.
- Comet Storm: Requires talent.
- Shimmer: Requires talent.
- Mirror Image: Requires talent.
- Alter Time: Requires talent.
- Supernova: Requires talent.

========================================================================
SPEC: ARCANE (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Arcane Charges (1-4) and Mana Management.
PRIORITY LIST (Burn Phase):
1. Arcane Power / Rune of Power: Activate first.
2. Arcane Blast: Build to 4 charges.
3. Arcane Missiles: Use when 2+ procs are active.
4. Arcane Blast: Maintain pressure.
5. Arcane Barrage: Use to reset charges and dump damage.

PRIORITY LIST (Conserve Phase):
1. Arcane Blast: Build to 2-3 charges.
2. Arcane Missiles: Use on procs.
3. Arcane Barrage: Use to prevent overcapping.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Clearcasting: Procs Arcane Missiles.
- Mark of Aluneth: High damage proc for Arcane Blast.

COOLDOWNS:
- Arcane Power: Massive damage increase.
- Presence of Mind: Instant cast for next spell.

========================================================================
SPEC: FIRE (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Hot Streak (Guaranteed Critical Pyroblast/Fire Blast).
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Combustion: Primary burst window.
2. Pyroblast: Use during Hot Streak.
3. Fire Blast: Use to generate Hot Streak.
4. Fireball / Frostfire Bolt: Filler to build Hot Streak.
5. Scorch: Use when moving.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Hot Streak: Triggered by critical hits.

COOLDOWNS:
- Combustion: Guarantees crits.
- Fire Keel / Trinkets: Use during Combustion.

========================================================================
SPEC: FROST (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Fingers of Frost and Winter's Chill.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Frozen Orb: Use on cooldown.
2. Ray of Frost / Comet Storm: High priority burst.
3. Flurry: Use with Brain Freeze proc.
4. Ice Lance: Use with Fingers of Frost or Winter's Chill.
5. Frostbolt: Filler to generate procs.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Fingers of Frost: Procs Ice Lance.
- Brain Freeze: Procs Flurry.
- Winter's Chill: Applied by Flurry/Frostbolt.

COOLDOWNS:
- Icy Veins: Increases cast speed and damage.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL MAGE)
========================================================================
- Opener: Root/Slow (Frost Nova/Slow) -> Burst.
- Utility: Counterspell (Interrupt), Polymorph (CC), Blink/Shimmer (Mobility).
- Arcane PvP: Focus on burst and kiting. Use Arcane Barrage for high damage.
- Fire PvP: High burst, use Combustion to melt targets. Use Pyroblast to finish.
- Frost PvP: Heavy control and kiting. Maintain slows and freezes.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-110)
========================================================================
- Level 1-10: Basic spells, Frostbolt, Fireball.
- Level 10+: Spec into Frost for faster AoE and survivability.
- General Loop: Frostbolt $\rightarrow$ Ice Lance (on proc) $\rightarrow$ Flurry.
- Use Mirror Image to distract mobs during high-pulls.

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Goal
Build PsychoBuddy, a professional-grade, standalone multi-boxing and automation platform for World of Warcraft, specifically targeting versions 7.3.5 (Legion) and 8.3.7 (BFA). The software is designed to allow a user to play one main account manually while autonomously controlling a "fleet" of other clients (typically 4+) in the background.

Instructions
Positioning: PsychoBuddy is a standalone consumer product (like WRobot or HonorBuddy), not a module.
Legal/Ethical Constraints:
Use on official Blizzard servers is STRICTLY PROHIBITED.
Intended exclusively for approved private servers and personal/local servers.
Must include a strong disclaimer that the author is not liable for account bans.
Communication Style: Direct and technical.
License: Proprietary "Source-Available Development & Evaluation License" authored by [Psychostout]. Commercial use and unauthorized distribution are prohibited.
Discoveries
Technical Architecture
The Senses (Data Acquisition):
Power Mode: Memory reading via P/Invoke ReadProcessMemory using a C++ DLL. Employs Pattern Scanning to automatically find offsets after patches.
Stealth Mode: Pixel sensing. A companion Lua addon encodes game state into a 10x10 pixel grid; the bot decodes this via DXGI or Windows Graphics Capture (WGC).
The Brain (Decision Engine):
Priority Rotation Engine: A requirement-based priority queue (e.g., IF Proc_A == Active THEN Cast Spell_A). Essential for handling the complex procs and artifact abilities of Legion/BFA.
Behavior System: A modular "If-Then" framework for high-level tasks (e.g., IF Health < 30% THEN Execute(HealBehavior)).
Navigation: A* Pathfinding over triangle meshes extracted from MPQ files.
The Muscles (Input Simulation):
Multi-boxing Engine: Uses Win32 API PostMessage and SendMessage to send inputs to background windows without taking focus.
Hardware Bypass: Integration with Arduino Leonardo or Raspberry Pi Pico as USB HID devices for hardware-level keypresses.
Bypass Logic: Use of RunMacroText for servers with modified kernels.
Attachment System:
Smart Attachment: Scans for Wow.exe, identifies instances via Window Titles (Character Names), and provides a fallback for custom executable paths (e.g., wow8.3.7.exe).
Binding: Maps PID and HWND to specific bot profiles.
Version Specifics (7.3.5 & 8.3.7)
Memory Offsets: Identified key structures for UnitFields, PlayerFields, and ObjectFields.

BFA PALADIN (8.3.7) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
TALENT-DEPENDENT ABILITIES (CHECK BEFORE CASTING)
========================================================================
- Beacon of Virtue: High HPS, high mana cost.
- Beacon of Faith: Stable, low mana cost.
- Divine Toll: Massive HP generation.
- Wake of Ashes: Burst damage and HP generation.
- Final Verdict: High single-target damage spender.

========================================================================
SPEC: HOLY (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Balancing Holy Power generation (Holy Shock/Flash of Light) with spending (Light of Dawn/Eternal Flame).
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Holy Shock: Primary generator and spot heal.
2. Light of Dawn: Primary HP spender for raid/group.
3. Eternal Flame: HP spender for single target/focused healing.
4. Flash of Light: Filler generator.
5. Judgment: Maintain buff and generate resources.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Beacon of Faith/Virtue: Maintain on primary tank.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avenging Wrath: Major burst healing window.
- Divine Toll: Instant HP generation.

========================================================================
SPEC: PROTECTION (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Generating Holy Power to fuel Shield of the Righteous for damage reduction.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Hammer of Wrath: Highest priority generator.
2. Judgment: Secondary generator.
3. Avenger's Shield: Third priority generator.
4. Hammer of the Righteous / Blessed Hammer: Filler generator.
5. Shield of the Righteous: Spend HP @ 3-5 charges to avoid overcapping.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Consecration: Maintain uptime for mitigation and damage.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avenging Wrath: Damage and mitigation boost.
- Divine Toll: Burst HP generation.
- Hammer of Light: High impact burst.

========================================================================
SPEC: RETRIBUTION (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Holy Power management. Maximize generators and avoid overcapping before using high-impact spenders.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Blade of Justice: Priority generator.
2. Judgment: Secondary generator.
3. Hammer of Wrath: Use on cooldown / execute phase.
4. Final Verdict: Primary ST spender (@ 5 HP).
5. Divine Storm: Primary AoE spender (@ 5 HP).

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Art of War: Procs instant Hammer of Wrath.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avenging Wrath: Primary burst window.
- Divine Toll: Instant HP generation.
- Wake of Ashes: Massive burst and HP generation.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL PALADIN)
========================================================================
- Utility: Hand of Freedom, Hand of Protection, Rebuke.
- Defensive: Divine Shield, Lay on Hands.
- Ret PvP: Burst rotation: Wings -> Wake of Ashes -> Divine Toll -> Final Verdict spam.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-120)
========================================================================
- Recommendation: Retribution.
- Loop: Judgment -> Blade of Justice -> Final Verdict.
- Use Divine Storm for 3+ mobs.
Open
legion_paladin.txt
PLAIN
LEGION PALADIN (7.3.5) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
TALENT-DEPENDENT ABILITIES (CHECK BEFORE CASTING)
========================================================================
- Grant Faith: High priority heal.
- Blessed Hammer: AoE heal/damage.
- Rule of Law: Range increase for heals and Mastery.
- Divine Purpose: Procs costless HP spenders.
- Judgment of Light: Heals attackers.

========================================================================
SPEC: HOLY (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Holy Power generation and spending for powerful burst heals.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Holy Shock: Primary generator and spot heal.
2. Crusade: Major cooldown for haste and damage/healing increase.
3. Light of Dawn: Primary HP spender for group healing.
4. Word of Glory: HP spender for single-target emergency healing.
5. Flash of Light: Filler generator.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Divine Purpose: Makes next HP spender costless.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avenging Wrath: Massive increase to crit and healing.
- Divine Protection: Personal survivability.

========================================================================
SPEC: PROTECTION (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Active mitigation (Shield of the Righteous) and AoE threat.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Avenger's Shield: Primary threat and mitigation.
2. Judgment: Maintain debuff and generate resources.
3. Blessed Hammer: Use if talented for AoE.
4. Consecration: Maintain uptime for damage reduction.
5. Hammer of the Righteous: Filler threat.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Shield of the Righteous: Use as soon as resources allow to maintain DR.

COOLDOWNS:
- Light of the Protector: Strongest heal (use < 60% HP).
- Guardian of Ancient Kings: Major defensive "wall".
- Vengeful Wrath: Burst damage/threat.

========================================================================
SPEC: RETRIBUTION (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Holy Power generators and high-impact spenders.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Judgment: Keep on cooldown (highest priority).
2. Justicar's Vengeance: Use on Divine Purpose proc @ 5 HP.
3. Templar's Verdict: Use @ 5 HP (or 4 if Judgment is up).
4. Crusader Strike / Zeal: Use @ 2 charges (Generator).
5. Blade of Justice: Use @ 3 HP or less (Generator).
6. Consecration: Maintain if talented.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Divine Purpose: Procs costless spender.

COOLDOWNS:
- Avenging Wrath: Primary burst window.
- Execution Sentence: High damage cooldown.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL PALADIN)
========================================================================
- Utility: Hand of Freedom (anti-slow), Hand of Protection (physical immunity), Rebuke (interrupt).
- Defensive: Divine Shield (Bubble), Lay on Hands (Full heal).
- Ret PvP: Burst with Wings -> Templar's Verdict -> Hammer of Wrath.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-110)
========================================================================
- Recommendation: Retribution for fastest soloing.
- Loop: Judgment -> Blade of Justice -> Templar's Verdict.
- Use Consecration for AoE pulls.

PLAIN
BFA PRIEST (8.3.7) - DATA SHEET

========================================================================
TALENT-DEPENDENT ABILITIES (CHECK BEFORE CASTING)
========================================================================
- Divine Toll: Massive Holy Power / Resource generation.
- Wake of Ashes: Burst damage (Shadow).
- Beacon of Virtue: High throughput, high mana.
- Power Infusion: Essential haste buff.
- Halo: AoE burst.

========================================================================
SPEC: HOLY (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Managing Holy Words and Prayer of Mending.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Holy Word: Serenity: Primary target heal.
2. Holy Word: Sanctify: Primary AoE heal.
3. Prayer of Mending: High priority for throughput.
4. Flash Heal: Filler (especially with Surge of Light).
5. Prayer of Healing: Group heal.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Surge of Light: Instant/Free Flash Heal.

COOLDOWNS:
- Divine Hymn: Massive group healing.
- Apotheosis: Enhanced healing throughput.
- Guardian Spirit: Emergency save.

========================================================================
SPEC: DISCIPLINE (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Atonement and resource-driven healing.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Power Word: Shield: Apply Atonement.
2. Penance: Primary Atonement tool.
3. Power Word: Radiance: AoE Atonement.
4. Smite / Holy Fire: Filler for Atonement healing.
5. Shadow Word: Pain: Maintain for damage.

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Atonement: Essential for any healing to occur.

COOLDOWNS:
- Divine Toll: Resource burst.
- Power Word: Barrier: Damage reduction.
- Rapture: Shield-based burst heal.

========================================================================
SPEC: SHADOW (PVE)
========================================================================
CORE MECHANIC: Insanity management and Void Eruption windows.
PRIORITY LIST:
1. Vampiric Touch: Maintain on all targets.
2. Mind Blast: Primary generator.
3. Devouring Plague: Primary spender.
4. Shadow Word: Death: Execute (< 20% health).
5. Mind Flay / Mind Sear: Filler (ST / AoE).

PROCS/CONDITIONALS:
- Voidform: Priority shifts to Void Bolt on cooldown.

COOLDOWNS:
- Void Eruption: Enter Voidform.
- Mindbender: Burst damage and resource gen.
- Power Infusion: Haste burst.

========================================================================
PVP LOGIC (GENERAL PRIEST)
========================================================================
- Utility: Psychic Scream, Mass Dispel, Mind Control.
- Defensive: Fade, Power Word: Shield.
- Shadow PvP: Use Mindbender and Voidform for burst pressure.

========================================================================
LEVELING (1-120)
========================================================================
- Recommendation: Shadow.
- Loop: Vampiric Touch -> Mind Blast -> Devouring Plague.
- Use Void Eruption on cooldown.
